     raise ValueError(f\"Unexpected numeric date value: {v!r}\")\n    if isinstance(v, str):\n        s = v.strip()\n        # support YYYY-MM-DD\n        try:\n            return datetime.strptime(s, '%Y-%m-%d').date()\n        except ValueError:\n            pass\n        # support YYYY/MM/DD\n        try:\n            return datetime.strptime(s, '%Y/%m/%d').date()\n        except ValueError:\n            pass\n    raise ValueError(f\"Unrecognized date value: {v!r} ({type(v)})\")\n\n\ndef iso(d):\n    return d.isoformat() if d else None\n\n\ndef find_header_row(ws, required_headers, search_rows=30):\n    req = {h.strip().lower() for h in required_headers}\n    for r in range(1, search_rows + 1):\n        row = [ws.cell(r, c).value for c in range(1, ws.max_column + 1)]\n        lowered = [str(v).strip().lower() for v in row if v is not None]\n        if req.issubset(set(lowered)):\n            # build header -> col index map\n            mapping = {}\n            for c, v in
